30 /**
31 * @brief Interface to the BLAS/cuBLAS `%ger` and `geru` routine.
32 *
33 * @details This function performs the rank 1 operation
34 * \f[
35 * \mathbf{A} \leftarrow \alpha \mathbf{x} \mathbf{y}^T + \mathbf{A} \; ,
36 * \f]
37 * where \f$ \alpha \f$ is a scalar, \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ is an \f$ m \f$ element vector, \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ is an \f$ n
38 * \f$ element vector and \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is an \f$ m \times n \f$ matrix.
39 *
40 * The vector \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ is never conjugated. For complex vectors, it calls `geru`. Use nda::blas::gerc to
41 * conjugate \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$.
42 *
43 * If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, the cuBLAS implementation is used.
44 *
45 * @tparam X nda::blas_lapack::BlasArray<1> type.
46 * @tparam Y nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<X, 1> type.
47 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<X, 2> type.
48 * @param alpha Input scalar \f$ \alpha \f$.
49 * @param x Input vector \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ of size \f$ m \f$.
50 * @param y Input vector \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ of size \f$ n \f$.
51 * @param a Input/Output matrix \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ of size \f$ m \times n \f$ to which the outer product is added.
52 */
53 template <BlasArray<1> X, BlasArrayFor<X, 1> Y, BlasArrayFor<X, 2> A>
54 150 void ger(get_value_t<X> alpha, X const &x, Y const &y, A &&a) { // NOLINT (temporary views are allowed here)
55 // for C-layout arrays/views, call ger with the transpose and swap x and y
56 if constexpr (has_C_layout<A>) {
57

77 /**
78 * @brief Interface to the BLAS/cuBLAS `%gerc` routine.
79 *
80 * @details This function performs the rank 1 operation
81 * \f[
82 * \mathbf{A} \leftarrow \alpha \mathbf{x} \mathbf{y}^H + \mathbf{A} \; ,
83 * \f]
84 * where \f$ \alpha \f$ is a scalar, \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ is an \f$ m \f$ element vector, \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ is an \f$ n
85 * \f$ element vector and \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ is an \f$ m \times n \f$ matrix.
86 *
87 * For real vectors/matrices, it calls nda::blas::ger.
88 *
89 * If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, the cuBLAS implementation is used.
90 *
91 * @tparam X nda::blas_lapack::BlasArray<1> type.
92 * @tparam Y nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<X, 1> type.
93 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<X, 2> type with nda::F_layout.
94 * @param alpha Input scalar \f$ \alpha \f$.
95 * @param x Input vector \f$ \mathbf{x} \f$ of size \f$ m \f$.
96 * @param y Input vector \f$ \mathbf{y} \f$ of size \f$ n \f$.
97 * @param a Input/Output matrix \f$ \mathbf{A} \f$ of size \f$ m \times n \f$ to which the outer product is added.
98 */
99 template <BlasArray<1> X, BlasArrayFor<X, 1> Y, BlasArrayFor<X, 2> A>
100 requires(has_F_layout<A>)

102 // check the dimensions of the input/output arrays/views
